==========================================================================================
HRS 2002 Final Imputation File, Version 1
ODBC Reference: [HRS02_1IMP]
Perl script: CodebkC1.pl v1.00 Mon Oct 24 12:32:12 2005

Note to Users: This codebook describes the imputation data for the Health and 
Retirement Study 2002 (HRS2002 Final). If you find any problems with the data, 
please report them to us at hrsquest@isr.umich.edu. 

Printing recommendation: Set margins (left/right/top/bottom) to .5"; print in 
portrait orientation using a mono-space 10-point font.

Although the basic imputation algorithm remains the same, the structure of the 
HRS imputation data in this release is different from that of the imputations for
a prior HRS/AHEAD wave. This is the case because the structure of the HRS core 
data has changed as a result of the changes in the survey instrument. For each 
variable to be imputed, we now only generate three imputation-related variables:
the 'X','C', and 'T' variables. In some cases, when data conversion is needed, 
we also create a 'Z' variable. The other two variables used to be in the 
imputation data, the 'D' and 'E' variables, are no longer necessary as they 
are now part of the HRS core data. For dicussions of our imputation strategy 
as well as the definitions of all the imputation-related variables, please  
check the documation file,'IMPUTE: A SAS Application System for Missing  
Value Imputations' at http://hrsonline.isr.umich.edu/docs/userg/dr-007.pdf. 
==========================================================================================
